0748 — smith bench press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0748 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supported_lying.

Lying on a bench, you press a smith machine bar up from your chest. Works the chest, shoulders and triceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the height of the smith machine bar to chest level. Lie flat on the bench with your feet firmly planted on the ground. Grip the bar with an overhand grip slightly wider than shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0748.json#/instructions/0, 0748.json#/instructions/1, 0748.json#/instructions/2

Arms Hands

Grip the bar with an overhand grip slightly wider than shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0748.json#/instructions/2

Legs Feet

Lie flat on the bench with your feet firmly planted on the ground.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0748.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Adjustable utility bench, Squat/power rack. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0748.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the height of the smith machine bar to chest level. 0748.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lie flat on the bench with your feet firmly planted on the ground. 0748.json#/instructions/1
  3. Grip the bar with an overhand grip slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. 0748.json#/instructions/2
  4. Unrack the bar and lower it towards your chest, keeping your elbows tucked in. 0748.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment when the bar touches your chest. 0748.json#/instructions/4
  6. Push the bar back up to the starting position, fully extending your arms. 0748.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0748.json#/instructions/6

0749 — smith bent knee good morning

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0749 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

On the smith machine, you bend your knees slightly and hinge forward with the bar on your upper back. Works the hamstrings, glutes and lower back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start by standing with your feet shoulder-width apart, toes pointing forward. Place the barbell across your upper back, resting it on your traps.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0749.json#/instructions/0, 0749.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start by standing with your feet shoulder-width apart, toes pointing forward. 0749.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place the barbell across your upper back, resting it on your traps. 0749.json#/instructions/1
  3. Bend your knees slightly and hinge forward at the hips, keeping your back straight. 0749.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower your torso until it is parallel to the ground, feeling a stretch in your hamstrings. 0749.json#/instructions/3
  5. Engage your glutes and hamstrings to raise your torso back up to the starting position. 0749.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0749.json#/instructions/5

0750 — smith chair squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0750 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

On the smith machine, you squat down until you touch a chair. Works the quads and glutes with a clear depth marker.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Adjust the height of the smith machine bar to a comfortable position. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, toes slightly turned out. Place the barbell across your upper back, resting it on your traps.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0750.json#/instructions/0, 0750.json#/instructions/1, 0750.json#/instructions/2

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 0750.json#/instructions/1

For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0750.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Adjust the height of the smith machine bar to a comfortable position. 0750.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, toes slightly turned out. 0750.json#/instructions/1
  3. Place the barbell across your upper back, resting it on your traps. 0750.json#/instructions/2
  4. Engage your core and keep your chest up as you slowly lower your body by bending your knees and hips. 0750.json#/instructions/3
  5. Continue lowering until your thighs are parallel to the ground, or as low as you can comfortably go. 0750.json#/instructions/4
  6. Pause for a moment, then push through your heels to return to the starting position. 0750.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0750.json#/instructions/6

0752 — smith deadlift

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0752 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

On the smith machine, you lift the bar from hip height or lower with a hip hinge. Works the glutes, hamstrings and lower back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Set up the smith machine with the bar at hip height. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, toes pointing slightly outward.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0752.json#/instructions/0, 0752.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 0752.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, toes pointing slightly outward.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0752.json#/instructions/1

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: no additional prop is established. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0752.json#/instructions/1

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set up the smith machine with the bar at hip height. 0752.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart, toes pointing slightly outward. 0752.json#/instructions/1
  3. Bend at the hips and knees, keeping your back straight and chest up, and grip the bar with an overhand grip slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. 0752.json#/instructions/2
  4. Engage your core and lift the bar by extending your hips and knees, keeping the bar close to your body. 0752.json#/instructions/3
  5. Stand up straight, fully extending your hips and knees. 0752.json#/instructions/4
  6. Lower the bar back down by bending at the hips and knees, maintaining control and keeping your back straight. 0752.json#/instructions/5
  7. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0752.json#/instructions/6

An Olympic lifting pull that drives the barbell from the floor to chest height with a powerful extension and shrug. It builds explosive power in the hips, legs and back.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0776.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and the barbell on the ground in front of you. 0776.json#/instructions/0
  2. Bend your knees and hinge at the hips to lower into a squat position, gripping the barbell with an overhand grip. 0776.json#/instructions/1
  3. Keep your back straight and chest up as you drive through your heels to lift the barbell off the ground, extending your hips and knees. 0776.json#/instructions/2
  4. As the barbell reaches hip level, explosively pull it upwards, shrugging your shoulders and pulling your elbows high and to the sides. 0776.json#/instructions/3
  5. As the barbell reaches its highest point, quickly drop underneath it, pulling yourself into a deep squat position. 0776.json#/instructions/4
  6. Catch the barbell overhead with your arms fully extended and your knees bent. 0776.json#/instructions/5
  7. Stand up by extending your hips and knees, keeping the barbell overhead. 0776.json#/instructions/6
  8. Lower the barbell back down to the starting position by reversing the movement. 0776.json#/instructions/7
  9. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0776.json#/instructions/8
